FEATURE-ReportEmptyMatch.md ===\n# FEATURE: Report Empty Match\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261024 V1 |\n| Description | Make every report write its header, report |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nWhen a filter matches no record, every report still writes its header. Reports with a TOTAL row write a zero TOTAL row. The summary report writes empty `first` and `last`. The failures report writes its header only. The `skipped` count still reflects the whole file.\n\n## Output\n\n- `summary`: header, then one row with `records` 0, `first` and `last` empty, token and cost totals zero, `cost_usd` `0.0000`, `minutes` `0.0`.\n- `commands`, `targets`, `models`, `daily`: header, then a TOTAL row with zeros, `0.0` minutes, and `0.0000` cost where those columns exist. The models TOTAL row has an empty model column.\n- `failures`: header only.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC empty-match-summary ===\nIntent: A filter matching no records makes the summary report write zero counts with empty first and last, and the skipped count is unchanged.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=python3; --target, --since, --until filters |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`ddkstat <report> [--target <name>] [--since <YYYY-MM-DD>] [--until <YYYY-MM-DD>] <log>` parses arguments, wires the filters, and writes the selected report to standard output.\n\n## Behavior\n\n- The first argument is the report name: one of `summary`, `commands`, `targets`, `models`, `daily`, `failures`.\n- Options follow the report name. The log path is the final argument, and exactly one is required.\n- `Run(args []string, stdout, stderr io.Writer) int` uses a local `flag.FlagSet` configured with `flag.ContinueOnError`.\n- Parsed `--target`, `--since`, and `--until` values are passed to the filters before the report function runs.\n- Usage-error handling is owned by `FEATURE-CliUsageErrors.md`. Unreadable-log handling is owned by `FEATURE-CliUnreadableLog.md`.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC cli-parse-dispatch-summary ===\nIntent: A valid summary invocation with one log path exits 0 and writes a header row starting with records.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=python3; scope=test\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nimport json\nimport os\nimport subprocess\nimport tempfile\n\nrecord = {\"schema_version\": 1, \"execution_id\": \"d-1\", \"started_at\": \"2026-10-14T01:00:00.000Z\",\n          \"status\": \"succeeded\", \"job\": {\"command_name\": \"build\", \"target\": \"T1\", \"llm\": \"claude\", \"model\": \"haiku\"},\n          \"result\": {\"returncode\": 0, \"timed_out\": False, \"stats\": {}}}\n\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as work:\n    log = os.path.join(work, \"llm.jsonl\")\n    with open(log, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        f.write(json.dumps(record) + \"\\n\")\n    result = subprocess.run(\n        [os.path.join(os.getcwd(), \"ddkstat\"), \"summary\", log],\n        capture_output=True, text=True,\n    )\n    print(result.stdout)\n    print(result.stderr)\n    assert result.returncode == 0, (result.returncode, result.stderr)\n    rows = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(result.stdout)))\n    assert rows[0][0] == \"records\", rows[0]\n    assert dict(zip(rows[0], rows[1]))[\"records\"] == \"1\", rows\n=== END AC cli-parse-dispatch-summary ===\n\n=== AC cli-parse-filter-target-applied ===\nIntent: The --target option is passed to the filter, so a summary limited to another target counts zero records.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=python3; `main` alone calls `os.Exit`.\n- The report name and option order follow the stated command form.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-CliUsageErrors.md ===\n# FEATURE: CLI Usage Errors\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261024 V1 |\n| Description | Map usage errors to exit code 2 with no standard output. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-CliParse.md |\n| Provides    | usage error exit 2 |\n| Consumes    | ddkstat command line |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nUsage errors exit with code `2`, write one diagnostic line to standard error, and write nothing to standard output.\n\n## Usage Errors\n\n- An unknown report name.\n- An unknown option.\n- A missing option value.\n- A missing or extra log path.\n- A `--since` or `--until` value that is not a real calendar date in `YYYY-MM-DD` form. Validation uses `time.Parse` with layout `2006-01-02`, which rejects impossible dates such as `2026-02-30`.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC usage-unknown-report ===\nIntent: An unknown report name exits 2 with empty standard output.\nSuite: scoped\nRequires: executable=python3;
